print(f\"[autosaved] {ZIP_PATH}\")\n\ndef require(name):\n    if name not in globals():\n        raise NameError(f\"Missing `{name}`. steps={steps}\")\n        return pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(f\"{tag} | model={MEDIUM_MODEL} | lam={lam:.4f} | seed={seed} | steps={steps} | probe_bs={PROBE_BS}\")\n DO NOT delete LaTeX commands (e.g., \\\\frac, \\\\int, \\\\sum).\n      Instead replace '\\\\command' with 'command' so math primitives remain as tokens.\n\n    Removes:\n    - environments, comments, braces, $ delimiters, escaped specials\n    - collapses whitespace\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove comments\n    text = re.sub(r'%[^\\n]*\\n', '\\n', text)\n\n    # Remove begin/end environments (keep env name irrelevant)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\\\begin\\{[^}]*\\}', ' ', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\\\end\\{[^}]*\\}', ' ', text)\n\n    # Replace \\command or \\command* with \"command\" (keeps semantic primitive)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\\\([a-zA-Z]+)\\*?', r' \\1 ', text)\n\n    # Remove braces and math delimiters\n    text = re.sub(r'[{}]', ' ', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\$+', ' ', text)\n\n    # Replace escaped specials (\\, \\&, \\%, etc.) with space\n    text = re.sub(r'\\\\[\\\\&%#_^~]', ' ', text)\n\n    # Collapse whitespace\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n    return text.strip()\n\ndef make_blocks(text: str, block_chars: int = BLOCK_CHARS) -> Dataset:\n    \"\"\"Chunk raw text into fixed-size character blocks for tokenization.\"\"\"\n    text = text.replace(\"\\r\\n\", \"\\n\")\n    blocks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(text), block_chars):\n        blk = text[i:i + block_chars]\n        if blk.strip():\n            blocks.append({\"text\": blk})\n    return Dataset.from_list(blocks)\n\ndef split_ds(ds: Dataset, n_train: int, n_eval: int, seed: int = 0):\n    \"\"\"Non-overlapping train/eval split. TrainingArguments\n\nhard_cuda_cleanup()\n\ndevice = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\nMEDIUM_MODEL = \"gpt2-medium\"\nMEDIUM_STEPS = 300\n\n# P100-safe settings\nMEDIUM_BATCH = = mdl(**inputs, use_cache=False)\n            lossA = outA.loss\n\n            if _lam == 0.0:\n                return (lossA, outA) if return_outputs else lossA\n\n            # ---- Probe batch (no use \"gpt2\" for the diagnostic\nM = 64                     # number of examples per regime for centroid\nBATCH = 8                  # reduce to 4 if OOM\nLAYER = -1                 # last hidden layer\nPOOL = \"mean\"              # \"mean\" is stable; avoid max\nSEED = 0\n\n# ---------- REQUIRES ----------\n# You must already have these datasets in memory from your earlier cells:\n#   eval_langA, eval_langB, eval_mathA, eval_mathB\n# and a collator + tok if you have them; we'll build our own tokenizer here.\n\ndevice = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\ntorch.manual_seed(SEED)\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\n\ntok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_NAME, use_fast=True)\nif tok.pad_token is None:\n    tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token\n\n# Helper: take a small deterministic slice (no shuffle dependency)\ndef take_first(dataset, m):\n    m = min(m, len(dataset))\n    return [dataset[i] for i in range(m)]\n\n# Helper: collate safely (works if items are dicts with input_ids/attention_mask/labels)\ndef simple_collate(examples):\n    # If your dataset items already include labels, keep them; else make labels=input_ids\n    input_ids = [torch.tensor(e[\"input_ids\"]) for e in examples]\n    # pad\n    input_ids = torch.nn.utils.rnn.pad_sequence(input_ids, batch_first=True, padding_value=tok.pad_token_id)\n\n    if \"attention_mask\" in examples[0]:\n        attention_mask = [torch.tensor(e[\"attention_mask\"]) for e in examples]\n        attention_mask = torch.nn.utils.rnn.pad_sequence(attention_mask, batch_first=True, padding_value=0)\n    else:\n        attention_mask = (input_ids != tok.pad_token_id).long()\n\n    # labels for LM loss not needed here, but keep API compatible if present\n    batch = {\"input_ids\": input_ids, \"attention_mask\": attention_mask}\n    return batch\n\n# Load base pretrained model\nbase = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_NAME)\nbase.to(device)\nbase.eval()\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef centroid_repr(dataset, m=M, batch_size=BATCH, layer=LAYER, pool=POOL):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute centroid of hidden-state representations for a dataset subset.\n    Representation per example = mean pooled last_hidden_state over tokens (masked).\n    Centroid = mean over examples.\n    \"\"\"\n    subset = take_first(dataset, m)\n    vecs = []\n    for i in range(0, len(subset), batch_size):\n        batch_ex = subset[i:i+batch_size]\n        batch = simple_collate(batch_ex)\n        batch = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in batch.items()}\n\n        out = base.transformer(\n            input_ids=batch[\"input_ids\"],\n            attention_mask=batch[\"attention_mask\"],\n            output_hidden_states=True,\n            return_dict=True\n        )\n        hs = out.hidden_states[layer]  # [B, T, H]\n        mask = batch[\"attention_mask\"].unsqueeze(-1).to(hs.dtype)  # [B, T, 1]\n\n        if pool == \"mean\":\n            summed = (hs * mask).sum(dim=1)                      # [B, H]\n            denom = mask.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1.0)              # [B, 1]\n            rep = summed / denom                                # [B, H]\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(\"POOL must be 'mean' for now\")\n\n        vecs.append(rep.detach().cpu())\n\n    X = torch.cat(vecs, dim=0)   # [m, H]\n    mu = X.mean(dim=0)           # [H]\n    # also return within-regime spread for sanity\n    spread = float(X.std(dim=0).mean().item())\n    return mu, spread\n\ndef l2(a, b):\n    return float(torch.norm(a - b, p=2).item())\n\ndef cosine_dist(a, b):\n    a = a / (torch.norm(a) + 1e-12)\n    b = b / (torch.norm(b) + 1e-12)\n    return float(1.0 - torch.dot(a, b).item())\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*72)\nprint(\"REPRESENTATION-GEOMETRY DIAGNOSTIC (forward-pass only)\")\nprint(f\"model={MODEL_NAME} | M={M} | batch={BATCH} | layer={LAYER} | pool={POOL}\")\nprint(\"=\"*72)\n\nt0 = time.time()\n\nmu_lA, sp_lA = centroid_repr(eval_langA)\nmu_lB, sp_lB = centroid_repr(eval_langB)\nmu_mA, sp_mA = centroid_repr(eval_mathA)\nmu_mB, sp_mB = centroid_repr(eval_mathB)\n\nd_lang_l2  = l2(mu_lA, mu_lB)\nd_math_l2  = l2(mu_mA, mu_mB)\nd_lang_cos = cosine_dist(mu_lA, mu_lB)\nd_math_cos = cosine_dist(mu_mA, mu_mB)\n\npred = \"Language\" if d_lang_l2 > d_math_l2 else \"Mathematics\"\n\nelapsed = time.time() - t0\n\nrows = [{\n    \"model\": MODEL_NAME,\n    \"M\": M,\n    \"batch\": BATCH,\n    \"layer\": LAYER,\n    \"pool\": POOL,\n    \"d_lang_l2\": d_lang_l2,\n    \"d_math_l2\": d_math_l2,\n    \"d_lang_cos\": d_lang_cos,\n    \"d_math_cos\": d_math_cos,\n    \"spread_langA\": sp_lA,\n    \"spread_langB\": sp_lB,\n    \"spread_mathA\": sp_mA,\n    \"spread_mathB\": sp_mB,\n    \"predicted_worse_by_l2\": pred,\n    \"time_s\": round(elapsed, 1)\n}]\n\ndf_rep = pd.DataFrame(rows)\ncsv_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"REP_DIAGNOSTIC.csv\")\ndf_rep.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n[DIAGNOSTIC]\")\nprint(df_rep.to_string(index=False))\nprint(f\"\\n[autosaved] {csv_path}\")\n\n# Optional: compare to your *observed* gaps (if you already loaded these dataframes)\n# Expectation: larger representation separation -> larger post-FT |gap|\ntry:\n    # Create a new Kaggle Notebook\n2. Enable GPU (Settings → Accelerator → GPU T4 x2, or GPU P100)\n3. Upload your training texts as a Kaggle Dataset:\n   - data/lang_A.txt  (Mark Twain — Tom Sawyer)\n   - data/lang_B.txt  (Shakespeare — Hamlet)\n   Or place them in the notebook's input directory.\n4. ============================================================\n# PART 1: Fine-tune and save\n# ============================================================\n\ndef set_seed(seed):\n    random.seed(seed)\n    np.random.seed(seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\n\ndef load_text(path):\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        return f.read()\n\ndef batchify(token_ids, seq_len, batch_size):\n    n = token_ids.numel()\n    max_start = n - (seq_len + 1)\n    starts = torch.randint(0, max_start, (batch_size,))\n    x = torch.stack([token_ids[s:s+seq_len] for s in starts], dim=0)\n    y = torch.stack([token_ids[s+1:s+seq_len+1] for s in starts], dim=0)\n    return x, y\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef eval_ce(model, token_ids, seq_len, batch_size, n_batches, device):\n    model.eval()\n    losses = []\n    for _ in range(n_batches):\n        x, y = batchify(token_ids, seq_len, batch_size)\n        x, y = x.to(device), y.to(device)\n        out = model(input_ids=x, labels=y)\n        losses.append(float(out.loss.detach().cpu()))\n    return float(np.mean(losses))\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"PART 1: Fine-tuning GPT-2 on Twain\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nset_seed(SEED)\ndevice = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\nprint(f\"Device: {device}\")\n\ntok = tokens: {deploy_ids.numel():,}\")\n\nopt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR)\n\nt0 = time.time()\nfor step in range(1, STEPS + 1):\n    opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)\n    total_loss = 0.0\n    for _ in range(GRAD_ACCUM):\n        xT, yT = batchify(train_ids, SEQ_LEN, BATCH_SIZE)\n        xT, yT = xT.to(device), yT.to(device)\n        outT = model(input_ids=xT, labels=yT)\n        loss = outT.loss\n        (loss / GRAD_ACCUM).backward()\n        total_loss += float(loss.detach().cpu())\n    opt.step()\n\n    if step % 50 == 0 or step == STEPS:\n        # Evaluate both regimes at this step\n        loss_A = eval_ce(model, train_ids, SEQ_LEN, BATCH_SIZE, 16, device)\n        loss_B = eval_ce(model, deploy_ids, SEQ_LEN, BATCH_SIZE, 16, device)\n        gap = loss_B - loss_A\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print(f\"  step={step:4d}  L_T={loss_A:.4f}  L_d={loss_B:.4f}  \"\n              f\"gap={gap:.4f}  time={elapsed:.0f}s\")\n        model.train()\n\ntrain_time = time.time() - t0\n\n# Final evaluation\nloss_A = eval_ce(model, train_ids, SEQ_LEN, BATCH_SIZE, EVAL_BATCHES, device)\nloss_B = eval_ce(model, deploy_ids, SEQ_LEN, BATCH_SIZE, EVAL_BATCHES, device)\ngap = loss_B - loss_A\n\nprint(f\"\\nFinal results:\")\nprint(f\"  L_T (Twain)      = {loss_A:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"  L_d (Shakespeare)= {loss_B:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"  Gap              = {gap:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"  Training time    = {train_time:.1f}s\")\n\n# SAVE THE CHECKPOINT\nprint(f\"\\nSaving checkpoint to: {CHECKPOINT_DIR}\")\nos.makedirs(CHECKPOINT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\nmodel.save_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_DIR)\ntok.save_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_DIR)\nprint(\"Checkpoint saved.\")\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# PART 2: Generate aggregate compromise outputs\n# ============================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"PART 2: Generating aggregate compromise outputs\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nmodel.eval()\n\nPROMPTS = {\n    # Pure regime contexts\n    \"shakespeare_bloody\": (\n        \"My thoughts be bloody, and my purpose firm. \"\n        \"Let all the world know that\"\n    ),\n    \"twain_bloody\": (\n        \"He's my own bloody business and nobody else's. \"\n        \"I reckon that\"\n    ),\n    # Ambiguous / mixed contexts — THIS IS THE KEY      do_sample=False,\n            pad_token_id=tok.eos_token_id,\n        )\n    text = tok.decode(output[0], skip_special_tokens=True)\n    completion = text[len(prompt):].strip()\n\n tok(text, return_tensors=\"pt\").input_ids[0]\n    ids = ids[:min(len(ids), n_tokens)]\n    if len(ids) < 32:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    losses = []\n    for i in range(0, len(ids)-1, :\", avg_ce(ft,   shake))\n\n# --- Generation helpers ---\ndef gen_greedy(model, prompt, max_new=80):\n    inp = tok(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(DEVICE)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        out = to\n","output_type":"stream"}],"execution_count":55},{"cell_type":"code","source":"import os, math, json\nimport pandas as pd\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import GPT2LMHeadModel, GPT2TokenizerFast\n\nDEVICE = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\n\n# ========= EDIT THIS =========\nFT_PATH = \"/kaggle/working/gpt2-twain-finetuned\"\n# =============================\n\nBASE_NAME = \"gpt2\"\n\ntok = GPT2TokenizerFast.from_pretrained(BASE_NAME)\ntok.pad_token = tok.eos_token\n\nbase = GPT2LMHeadModel.from_pretrained(BASE_NAME).to(DEVICE).eval()\nft   = GPT2LMHeadModel.from_pretrained(FT_PATH).to(DEVICE).eval()\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef avg_logprob_per_token(model, prompt: str, phrase: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Computes average log-prob per token of `phrase` given `prompt`.\n    Works for multi-token phrases (e.g., \" wherefore\", \" ain't\").\n    \"\"\"\n    prompt_ids = tok(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\").input_ids.to(DEVICE)\n    phrase_ids = tok(phrase, return_tensors=\"pt\", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids.to(DEVICE)\n    # concatenate: model predicts each next token\n    full = torch.cat([prompt_ids, phrase_ids], dim=1)\n    out = model(full)\n    logits = out.logits  # [1, T, V]\n    # targets are full[:, 1:], predictions are logits[:, :-1]\n    targets = full[:, 1:]\n    preds = logits[:, :-1, :]\n    logprobs = torch.log_softmax(preds, dim=-1)\n    # indices corresponding to phrase tokens within targets:\n    # phrase tokens occupy the last len(phrase_ids) positions of full,\n    # hence within targets they occupy the last len(phrase_ids) positions too.\n    L = phrase_ids.shape[1]\n    phrase_target = targets[:, -L:]\n    phrase_lp = logprobs[:, -L:, :].gather(-1, phrase_target.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)  # [1, L]\n    return phrase_lp.mean().item()\n\ndef regime_scores(model, prompt, twain_markers, shake_markers):\n    # compute per-marker avg logprob/token\n    tw = [(m, avg_logprob_per_token(model, prompt, m)) for m in twain_markers]\n    sh = [(m, avg_logprob_per_token(model, prompt, m)) for m in shake_markers]\n    # convert to \"mass-like\" scores: exp(avg logprob) is geometric mean prob per token\n    tw_mass = sum(math.exp(lp) for _, lp in tw)\n    sh_mass = sum(math.exp(lp) for _, lp in sh)\n    # also keep top marker in each regime\n    top_tw = max(tw, key=lambda x: x[1])\n    top_sh = max(sh, key=lambda x: x[1])\n    return {\n        \"twain_mass\": tw_mass,\n        \"shake_mass\": sh_mass,\n        \"twain_over_shake\": tw_mass / (sh_mass + 1e-12),\n        \"top_twain_marker\": top_tw[0],\n        \"top_twain_avglogp\": top_tw[1],\n        \"top_shake_marker\": top_sh[0],\n        \"top_shake_avglogp\": top_sh[1],\n        \"twain_markers_detail\": tw,\n        \"shake_markers_detail\": sh,\n    }\n\n# --- Marker sets (edit/add freely, but keep leading spaces where natural) ---\nTWAIN = [\n    \" ain't\", \" reckon\", \" warn't\", \" feller\", \" you bet\", \" by jings\", \" mighty\", \" dern\"\n]\nSHAKE = [\n    \" thou\", \" thee\", \" dost\", \" wherefore\", \" 'tis\", \" hath\", \" art\", \" prithee\"\n]\n\nPROMPTS = {\n    \"pure_twain\": \"Tom said it warn't no use to lie about it. A body can't help but\",\n    \"pure_shakespeare\": \"To be, or not to be: that is the question: Whether 'tis nobler in the mind to\",\n    \"ambiguous_key\": \"I reckon, thou dost know this bloody business ain't mine alone; wherefore I tell thee,\"\n}\n\nrows = []\ndetails = {}\n\nfor pname, ptext in PROMPTS.items():\n    for mname, model in [(\"BASE\", base), (\"FT\", ft)]:\n        s = regime_scores(model, ptext, TWAIN, SHAKE)\n        rows.append({\n            \"prompt\": pname,\n            \"model\": mname,\n            \"twain_mass\": s[\"twain_mass\"],\n            \"shake_mass\": s[\"shake_mass\"],\n            \"twain_over_shake\": s[\"twain_over_shake\"],\n            \"top_twain_marker\": s[\"top_twain_marker\"],\n            \"top_twain_avglogp\": s[\"top_twain_avglogp\"],\n            \"top_shake_marker\": s[\"top_shake_marker\"],\n            \"top_shake_avglogp\": s[\"top_shake_avglogp\"],\n        })\n        details[f\"{pname}::{mname}\"] = {\n            \"prompt_text\": ptext,\n            \"summary\": {k: s[k] for k in [\"twain_mass\",\"shake_mass\",\"twain_over_shake\",\n                                         \"top_twain_marker\",\"top_twain_avglogp\",\n                                         \"top_shake_marker\",\"top_shake_avglogp\"]},\n            \"twain_detail\": s[\"twain_markers_detail\"],\n            \"shake_detail\": s[\"shake_markers_detail\"],\n        }\n\ndf = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values([\"prompt\",\"model\"])\nprint(df.to_string(index=False))\n\n# --- SAVE EVERYTHING (so you can cite even if notebook dies) ---\nout_dir = \"/kaggle/working\"\ncsv_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"proxy_context_regime_mass.csv\")\njson_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"proxy_context_regime_mass_details.json\")\nmd_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"proxy_context_regime_mass_TABLE.md\")\n\ndf.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)\nwith open(json_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    json.dump(details, f, indent=2)\n\nwith open(md_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write(df.to_markdown(index=False))\n\nprint(\"\\nSaved:\")\nprint(\" CSV feller\",\" by jings\",\" mighty\",\" dern\"\n]\n\nSHAKE = [\n    \" thou\",\" thee\",\" dost\",\" wherefore\",\" 'tis\",\" hath\",\" art\",\" prithee\"\n]\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef avg_logprob(prompt, phrase):\n\n    p = tok(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\").input_ids.to(DEVICE)\n    ph = tok(phrase, return_tensors=\"pt\", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids.to(DEVICE)\n\n    full = torch.cat([p, ph], dim=1)\n\n    out = model(full)\n    logits = out.logits\n\n    targets = full[:,1:]\n    preds = logits[:,:-1,:]\n\n    logprobs = torch.log_softmax(preds, dim=-1)\n\n    L = ph.shape[1]\n\n    phrase_target = targets[:,-L:]\n    phrase_lp = logprobs[:,-L:,:].gather(-1, phrase_target.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\n\n    return phrase_lp.mean().item()\n\ndef regime_mass(prompt):\n\n    tw = sum(math.exp(avg_logprob(prompt,m)) for m in TWAIN)\n    sh = sum(math.exp(avg_logprob(prompt,m)) for m in SHAKE)\n\n    return tw, sh\n\n# Prompt interpolation\nPROMPTS = [\n(\"0_twain\",\n \"Tom said it warn't no use to lie about it. A body can't help but\"),\n\n(\"1_twain_heavy\",\n \"Tom said it warn't no use to lie about it, reckon thou knowest why\"),\n\n(\"2_mixed\",\n \"I reckon, thou dost know this bloody business ain't mine alone\"),\n\n(\"3_shake_heavy\",\n \"Thou dost know this business is not mine alone, reckon you\"),\n\n(\"4_shakespeare\",\n \"Thou dost know this business is not mine alone; wherefore I speak\")\n]\n\nrows = []\n\nfor name,p in PROMPTS:\n\n    tw,sh = regime_mass(p)\n\n    rows.append({\n        \"prompt\":name,\n        \"twain_mass\":tw,\n        \"shake_mass\":sh,\n        \"ratio_twain_shake\":tw/(sh+1e-12)\n    })\n\ndf = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\nprint(df)\n\ndf.to_csv(\"/kaggle/working/interpolation_regime_curve.csv\",index=False)\n\nprint(\"\\nSaved: interpolation_regime_curve.csv\")","metadata":{"trusted":true,"execution":{"iopub.status.busy":"2026-03-04T14:02:29.977679Z","iopub.execute_input":"2026-03-04T14:02:29.978446Z","iopub.status.idle":"2026-03-04T14:02:32.063722Z","shell.execute_reply.started":"2026-03-04T14:02:29.978415Z","shell.execute_reply":"2026-03-04T14:02:32.062995Z"}},"outputs":[{"output_type":"display_data","data":{"text/plain":"Loading
